1 According to the new urban definition, the urban population comprises persons residing in urban territory but not necessarily in an urban place, whiah is defined as an incor￾porated place of 2,500 or more, or an unincorporated place of 2,500 or more located outside an urbanizetl area. Under the old definition, incorporated places of 2,500 or more and places 
urban under spocial rule aro classified as urban places. There were no places urban under spccittl rule in New Mexico.

No estimate of population in 1850 is available for territory acquired from Mexico tbrough the Gadsden Purchase (1853), annexed to New Mexico in 1854.
